Consequently, current models often suffer from "semantic blindness," overfitting to transient noise while failing to leverage invariant soft biometrics, and struggle to capture temporal motion signatures. To bridge this gap, we propose SapiensID 2.0, a human recognition framework enriched with both semantic and temporal awareness. To overcome the lack of soft-biometric annotations, we transfer zero-shot semantic knowledge from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) into a discriminative embedding space. We resolve the dimensional mismatch between these spaces using Invariant Trait Alignment (ITA) to distill core persistent traits, and Transient Noise Disentanglement (TND) to decouple artifacts like clothing. Furthermore, we design a Kinematic Semantic Attention Head (K-SAH) that extends spatial attention across temporal windows. By tracking semantic patches over time, K-SAH captures rich kinematic signatures without requiring large-scale video datasets. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SapiensID 2.0 achieves state-of-the-art performance across image- and video-based person re-identification and gait recognition, while maintaining robust face recognition capabilities.

</details>


### [35] [DSAR: Dual-Stream Autoregressive Modeling of Temporal Cloth Dynamics for Photorealistic Animatable Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10500)
*Haozhong Xiong,Yao Yu,Yu Zhou,Sidan Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双流自回归框架，用于从RGB视频中创建逼真且时间一致的动画人类化身。该框架显式建模布料物理中的时间因果性，通过几何流传播表面位移、状态流融合历史状态信息，解决了现有方法因忽略时间因果性而导致的过度平滑和伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从RGB视频生成动画化身的性能受限于对布料动力学捕捉不足，产生过度平滑的外观或训练分布外姿态上的严重伪影。根本原因是现有方法忽视了布料物理中的时间因果性——当前状态源于先前状态的时间演化，而非仅由瞬时骨骼姿态决定。缺乏因果结构建模导致网络学习姿态-外观相关性而非运动演化，泛化能力差。

Method: 提出双流自回归框架：(1) 几何流（geometric stream）从前一帧传播表面位移，建模可观测的几何信息；(2) 状态流（state stream）将当前特征与从记忆库检索的历史状态融合，建模隐式内部状态。采用运动自适应聚合（motion-adaptive aggregation）处理空间变化的动力学，并用自适应正则化在平滑性与灵活性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在多个挑战性数据集上的实验表明，该方法在渲染质量、时间一致性和对训练分布外运动模式的泛化能力上均有显著提升，验证了双流时间建模能够实现逼真的布料动力学。

Conclusion: 显式建模双流时间因果关系（几何信息与隐式状态）是捕捉真实布料动力学的关键，该框架有效解决了现有方法在时间一致性和分布外泛化上的局限性。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic and temporally coherent animatable human avatars from RGB videos remains challenging. Current methods struggle to capture realistic cloth dynamics, producing over-smoothed appearance or severe artifacts on out-of-distribution poses. This limitation stems from a fundamental oversight: existing approaches neglect the temporal causality inherent in cloth physics, where current states emerge from previous states through temporal evolution rather than instantaneous skeletal configurations alone. Without explicit modeling of this causal structure, networks learn pose-appearance correlations instead of motion evolution, leading to poor generalization. We introduce a dual-stream autoregressive framework that explicitly models both observable geometric information and implicit internal state. The geometric stream propagates surface displacement from the previous frame, while the state stream fuses current features with historical states retrieved from a memory bank. Motion-adaptive aggregation handles spatially-varying dynamics, and adaptive regularization balances smoothness with flexibility. Experiments on challenging datasets demonstrate significant improvements in rendering quality, temporal consistency, and generalization to motion patterns beyond training distributions, validating that dual-stream temporal modeling enables realistic cloth dynamics.

</details>


### [36] [Towards Color-Faithful Low-Light Image Enhancement via Adaptive Color Debiasing and Saturation Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10512)
*Zhichen Yang,Rui Xu,Yuzhen Niu,Fusheng Li,Hui Da,Ri Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAGE，一个基于圆柱形颜色校正框架的低光图像增强方法，通过自适应的颜色去偏（AdaLAB色彩空间）和色域协调的饱和度校正（AdaCCT变换），在保持亮度恢复的同时实现更忠实、更自然的颜色还原。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光成像由于低信噪比和图像形成过程常引入颜色偏差，现有低光增强方法虽能恢复亮度，但颜色还原仍存在整体偏色以及局部欠饱和/过饱和的问题，因此需要一个专门的色彩校正框架来解决颜色失真。

Method: 提出CAGE框架，包括两个核心组件：(1) AdaLAB——圆柱形自适应LAB色彩空间，提供解耦且图像特异的统一颜色校正基；(2) AdaCCT——自适应圆柱形颜色变换，包含正向与逆向变换。正向变换在主干增强前通过色度平面平移和缩放重组色度分布以抑制嵌入的颜色偏置，逆向变换通过超出色域的亮度补偿实现忠实的饱和度校正。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，CAGE能够实现更忠实的颜色还原，显著降低颜色偏置和饱和度异常，并在不同的低光增强主干网络上均获得更好的整体视觉质量。

Conclusion: CAGE通过构建解耦的自适应色彩空间和双向圆柱形颜色变换，有效解决了低光图像增强中的颜色失真问题，可作为即插即用的颜色校正模块适配不同增强主干，代码已开源。

Abstract: Low-light imaging often introduces color bias caused by the low signal-to-noise ratio and the image formation process. Although recent low-light image enhancement methods have achieved strong brightness recovery, faithful color restoration remains challenging, manifesting as overall color bias together with local under- and over-saturation. To address this issue, we propose CAGE, a cylindrical color correction framework with adaptive color debiasing and gamut-harmonized saturation rectification for color-faithful low-light image enhancement. We first introduce AdaLAB, a cylindrical adaptive LAB color space that provides a decoupled and image-specific basis for uniform color correction. Building on this color space, we further develop AdaCCT, an adaptive cylindrical color transform with forward and inverse transforms for the conversion between RGB and AdaLAB color space, as well as necessary color debiasing and saturation rectification. The forward transform suppresses embedded color bias before backbone enhancement by reorganizing the chromatic distribution through chromatic-plane shifting and scaling, while the inverse transform achieves faithful saturation rectification through out-of-gamut lightness compensation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that CAGE achieves more faithful color restoration, specifically reduces color bias and saturation abnormality, and delivers better overall visual quality across different low-light enhancement backbones. The code is available at https://yangzhichen763.github.io/CAGE/.

</details>


### [37] [SafeCap: Improving LVLM Safety with Image Captioning Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10513)
*Caoyuan Ma,Wenpu Liu,Weichu Xie,Tian Gu,Shilei Zhao,Lingxi Min,Shuai Dong,Yuqi Xu,Ji Zhao,Ziyue Wang,Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Yongfu Zhu,Wenqi Shao,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeCap，一种通过"自我生成危险性相关描述"的强化学习框架来对齐大型视觉语言模型的安全行为。策略模型先生成有助于安全决策的图像描述，再生成最终答案，通过冻结的LLM判断描述是否导向安全对齐的决策来优化该描述。实验表明SafeCap在五个多模态安全基准上提升3.7-19.0个安全平均分，同时保持或提升视觉效用，并优于安全SFT、DPO和SafeGRPO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）虽然继承了语言骨干模型的安全对齐，但通过视觉输入仍然可以被越狱攻击（jailbreak attacks）绕过安全机制。现有方法缺乏一种机制，让模型在生成回答前主动暴露与安全相关的视觉线索，从而帮助模型更鲁棒地做出安全决策。

Method: SafeCap是一个基于强化学习的框架，核心思想是"描述中介的安全对齐"（caption-mediated alignment）。具体来说：1）训练一个策略模型，先输出与安全性相关的图像描述，再基于该描述生成最终答案；2）引入一个冻结的LLM作为安全裁判，评估该描述是否足以引导其做出安全对齐的决策；3）通过强化学习优化描述，使其能够揭示对安全响应生成至关重要的视觉信息，而非仅依赖直接的拒绝监督信号。协议名称为DirectCap。

Result: 在五个多模态安全基准和六个视觉效用基准上，SafeCap在四种模型设置下将安全平均分提升3.7-19.0分，同时保持或改善视觉效用。在匹配骨干网络和数据的有控对比中，SafeCap优于安全SFT、DPO和SafeGRPO等方法。

Conclusion: SafeCap证明了"描述中介的强化学习"（caption-mediated reinforcement learning）在多模态安全对齐中的有效性，即通过优化自我生成的安全相关描述，可以显著提升LVLMs抵御视觉越狱攻击的鲁棒性且不牺牲视觉任务能力。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that exploit visual inputs to bypass safety alignment inherited from their language backbones. We propose SafeCap, a reinforcement-learning framework that aligns LVLMs through learned self-captioning. SafeCap trains a policy model to first generate a safety-relevant image caption and then produce a final answer; the caption is further optimized by whether it enables a frozen LLM to reach a safety-aligned decision. This caption-mediated objective encourages the policy to expose visual cues relevant to safe response generation rather than relying solely on direct refusal supervision. Across five multimodal safety benchmarks and six vision-utility benchmarks, SafeCap substantially improves aggregate safety performance under its intended DirectCap protocol, with gains of 3.7-19.0 points in safety average across four model settings while maintaining comparable or improved vision utility. Under controlled comparisons on matched backbones and data, SafeCap outperforms safety SFT, DPO, and SafeGRPO, demonstrating the effectiveness of caption-mediated reinforcement learning for multimodal safety alignment.

</details>


### [38] [SparSTAR: Sparse Attention for SpaceTime AutoRegressive Video Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10519)
*Jongbeom Lee,Hyunwoo Yu,Jincheol Yang,Jaemin Choi,Suk-Ju Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SparSTAR，一种针对InfinityStar视频视觉自回归生成任务的免训练块稀疏注意力方法，通过在昂贵的尺度和注意力头层级动态选择连续关键块并只执行前向稀疏路径，在保持所有token和精修尺度的情况下实现约1.6倍端到端加速，同时保持VBench和重建保真度接近稠密基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InfinityStar将视觉自回归生成扩展到视频，采用图像和剪辑金字塔序列。然而，其不断变化的尺度（scale）和跨剪辑上下文导致后期尺度的注意力计算成本高昂，而直接从扩散模型或图像VAR模型中复用的稀疏模式在这种场景下不可靠。因此需要一种针对该特定设置的注意力稀疏化方法。

Method: SparSTAR是一种免训练（training-free）的块稀疏注意力方法。在昂贵的尺度和每个注意力头处，SparSTAR基于当前查询和键激活对连续键块进行评分，保留所需的条件上下文，并通过仅前向的稀疏路径执行选中的块。此外，论文通过分析剪辑内跨尺度一致性、跨剪辑边界的模式持续性以及跨越来越远尺度的复用质量退化，发现重要键块会移动，因此在每个目标尺度重新计算块选择比复用迁移掩码更可靠。

Result: 在720p文生视频和图生视频任务上，SparSTAR在保留每一个token和精修尺度的前提下实现了约1.6倍的端到端加速，且VBench指标和成对输出重建保真度均接近稠密的InfinityStar。

Conclusion: 论文表明，针对视频VAR特定架构设计的免训练块稀疏注意力可以显著降低Late-scale注意力开销，且动态重选块比复用迁移稀疏掩码更可靠，在保真度几乎不受影响的同时获得可观的加速收益。

Abstract: InfinityStar extends visual autoregressive generation to video through a sequence of image and clip pyramids. Its changing scale and cross-clip context, however, leave late-scale attention costly and make sparse patterns reused from diffusion or image VAR models unreliable. We introduce SparSTAR, a training-free block-sparse attention method tailored to this setting. At each expensive scale and attention head, SparSTAR scores contiguous key blocks from the current query and key activations, retains required conditioning context, and executes the selected blocks through a forward-only sparse path. We analyze cross-scale consistency within a clip, pattern persistence across clip boundaries, and quality degradation as reuse spans increasingly distant scales. Across these analyses, important key blocks shift, showing that recomputing block selection at each target scale is more reliable than reusing a transferred mask. On 720p text-to-video and image-to-video generation, SparSTAR preserves every token and refinement scale while providing about a 1.6x end-to-end speedup and maintaining VBench and paired-output reconstruction fidelity close to dense InfinityStar.

</details>


### [39] [Unlocking the Power of Medical Tabular Data via Semantic-Aware Multimodal Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10522)
*Yingsheng Liu,Haiming Li,Jingmin Zhu,Jiajun Sun,Victoria Mar,Monika Janda,H. Peter Soyer,Zongyuan Ge,Zhen Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个语义感知框架，用于医学视觉-表格多模态预训练，通过建模表格数据的二维结构（重要性感知自适应掩码结合软标签离散化模块）来提升医学表示学习的性能，在皮肤病学和眼科学数据集上达到新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态预训练方法将结构化临床表格视为扁平向量，使用不稳定连续回归目标，忽略了表格数据中语义相关的内在结构（特征间重要性层次和特征内连续性-离散性双重性），导致诊断表型未被充分利用。

Method: 提出语义感知框架：(1) Importance-Aware Adaptive Masking，针对特征间诊断重要性差异，构建无标签课程学习策略，优先突出关键特征；(2) Soft-Label Discretized Module，针对特征内连续-离散二重性，用稳定的分布匹配替代不稳定的数值回归，数学上保留顺序关系。

Result: 在大型皮肤病学数据集（SLICE-3D, HOP）和眼科学数据集（EyePACS）上取得SOTA表现，展现出优异的鲁棒性和跨领域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模表格数据的二维结构能够有效提升医学视觉-表格多模态预训练的效果，语义感知设计优于传统扁平化处理方法。

Abstract: While vision-language models dominate medical representation learning, unstructured text lacks the dense, quantitative diagnostic phenotypes inherent in structured clinical tables. However, existing multimodal pre-training methods underutilize this potential due to semantic-agnostic designs that treat tabular inputs as flat vectors and employ unstable continuous regression objectives. To overcome this, we propose a novel semantic-aware framework explicitly modeling the intrinsic two-dimensional structure of tabular data. First, addressing the inter-feature hierarchy of varying diagnostic importance, we introduce Importance-Aware Adaptive Masking to construct a label-free curriculum prioritizing salient features. Second, addressing the intra-feature continuity-discreteness duality, we propose a Soft-Label Discretized Module that replaces unstable numerical regression with stable distribution matching, thereby mathematically preserving ordinal relationships. Extensive experiments across large-scale dermatology (SLICE-3D, HOP) and ophthalmology (EyePACS) datasets establish a new state-of-the-art (SOTA), demonstrating exceptional robustness and cross-domain generalizability.

</details>


### [40] [Rethinking Text-Based Image Retrieval in Specific Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10524)
*Jingyang Tan,Sheng Yang,Yuanpeng Chen,Jian Wang,Nianjin Ye,Chen Xing,Lanpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对现有Text-based Image Retrieval (TBIR)基准假设查询与图像一一对应的局限性，设计了面向特定领域（如安防监控）的多匹配TBIR数据引擎DSMM-TBIR，构建了包含5万张监控图像和200个查询的SecMM-TBIR基准。同时提出Semantic-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT)框架，通过语义感知软标签监督（SASS）和模态内结构蒸馏（ISD）解决特定领域中对比学习的假阴性问题，在SecMM-TBIR上平均mAP@20提升7.8个点，且不牺牲通用域性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TBIR基准基于查询与图像单一匹配假设构建，无法反映特定领域（如安防监控）中一个查询对应多个相关候选图像的实际场景，导致模型在领域特定任务上的性能评估失真；同时，特定领域中vanilla对比学习面临严重假阴性问题，损害检索性能。

Method: 提出DSMM-TBIR数据引擎以构建多匹配TBIR基准SecMM-TBIR；提出SAFT框架，包含两个关键组件：Semantic-Aware Soft-Label Supervision (SASS)用于缓解假阴性导致的语义压缩，以及Intra-modal Structural Distillation (ISD)用于保持模态内部结构一致性。

Result: 在多种CLIP-like模型上的实验表明，SAFT相比标准图像-文本对比（ITC）微调在SecMM-TBIR上平均mAP@20提升7.8个点，同时通用域性能也得到提升。

Conclusion: SAFT为领域特定TBIR任务建立了有效范式，解决了多匹配场景下对比学习的假阴性问题；SecMM-TBIR基准和DSMM-TBIR数据引擎将开源以促进该方向进一步研究。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid advancement of vision-language representation learning, Text-based Image Retrieval (TBIR) has made notable progress. However, existing benchmarks are predominantly constructed on an exclusive single-match assumption between query and images. While effective in general scenarios, this assumption fails to reflect practical system performance in specific domains (e.g., surveillance), where a single query often corresponds to multiple relevant candidate images. To address this limitation, we design a Domain-Specific Multi-Match Text-based Image Retrieval (DSMM-TBIR) data engine. Leveraging this engine, we construct Security Multi-Match TBIR (SecMM-TBIR), a benchmark comprising 50k surveillance images with 200 comprehensive queries. Furthermore, we observe that vanilla contrastive learning in specific domains suffers from severe false negatives, forcing the model to push apart semantically similar pairs and thus degrading retrieval performance. We propose the Semantic-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT) framework to address semantic compression in specific domains, which incorporates Semantic-Aware Soft-Label Supervision (SASS) and Intra-modal Structural Distillation (ISD) to establish a promising paradigm for domain-specific TBIR tasks. Experiments across diverse CLIP-like models demonstrate that SAFT yields an average mAP@20 gain of 7.8 points on SecMM-TBIR over standard image-text contrastive (ITC) fine-tuning, while also improving general-domain performance. The entire benchmark will be released to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [41] [Dynamic Context Adapters: Efficiently Infusing History into Vision-and-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10525)
*Yuhang Song,Bor-Jiun Lin,Jiaxu Liu,Te-Chuan Chiu,Anh Nguyen,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出动态上下文适配器（DCA），一种用于预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）的新型上下文注入方法，通过固定大小的动态压缩记忆来保留历史语义，避免帧拼接带来的二次注意力复杂度和内存消耗，在长时序决策任务中实现超过25%的注意力FLOPs减少和13%的内存节省，同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在顺序决策任务中缺乏时间理解能力，直接拼接历史帧会导致二次注意力复杂度和过大内存消耗，而现有的时间压缩方法要么计算开销大要么造成严重的信息损失，需要在保持计算效率的同时赋予预训练VLM记忆能力。

Method: 提出动态上下文适配器（DCA），采用固定大小的动态压缩记忆来保留历史语义信息，无需帧拼接操作，通过将静态VLM与循环策略（recurrent policy）桥接，使预训练模型具备记忆能力同时保持计算效率。

Result: DCA实现了超过25%的注意力FLOPs减少和13%的内存节省，同时提升了长时序任务的性能表现。

Conclusion: DCA有效解决了VLM在顺序决策中历史上下文整合的计算效率与信息保持之间的矛盾，为预训练VLM赋予记忆能力提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Historical context integration presents a fundamental challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in sequential decision-making tasks. Current VLMs process visual inputs independently, which creates critical limitations for downstream applications that require temporal understanding. Direct incorporation of historical frames into Transformer inputs produces quadratic attention complexity and excessive memory consumption. Existing approaches suffer from significant drawbacks: computational inflation or substantial information loss through temporal compression. To address these challenges, we introduce Dynamic Context Adapter (DCA), a novel context injection approach for pretrained VLMs. Our method employs fixed-size, dynamically compressed memory to preserve historical semantics without frame concatenation. DCA bridges static VLMs and recurrent policies and enables memory capabilities in pretrained models while maintaining computational efficiency. DCA achieves over $25\%$ reduction in attention FLOPs and $13\%$ memory savings while improving performance on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [42] [Flow Straight to Reality: Perceptually Consistent Flow Matching for Efficient Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10544)
*Sangwoo Jo,Donggeun Ko,Jayeon Kang,Youngsang Kwak,Jaehwa Kwak,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PCFlow框架，通过连续最优传输参数化从退化观测到清晰目标的映射，使用隐一致流目标与隐一致感知损失联合优化失真与感知质量，并采用无冲突梯度投影策略平衡多目标优化，实现轻量级高效图像恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像恢复在失真与感知之间存在固有矛盾——最小化像素误差导致过度平滑，而优化感知真实度又会引入结构偏差。现有基于后验采样或多阶段生成的方法虽试图权衡，但计算昂贵且架构复杂，亟需一种统一且高效的解决方案。

Method: PCFlow直接参数化从退化图像到清晰图像的连续传输流，包含三大组件：(1) 隐一致流目标驱动稳定高效的少步推理；(2) 隐一致感知损失(LCPL)在引导速度场上施加语义约束，引导动力学走向视觉锐利的数据流形；(3) 无冲突梯度投影策略稳定结构一致性与感知一致性之间的多目标优化景观。骨干网络采用轻量级纯卷积架构。

Result: 在多种图像恢复任务上取得具有竞争力的性能，同时将计算成本降低至传统方法的一小部分。

Conclusion: PCFlow证明了通过统一流匹配框架联合优化失真与感知质量是可行且高效的，以简化架构实现低成本高性能的图像恢复，为失真-感知权衡提供了新的解决路径。

Abstract: Image restoration is fundamentally constrained by the tradeoff between distortion and perception: minimizing pixel-wise error yields over-smoothed results, whereas optimizing for perceptual realism often introduces structural deviations. Recent approaches attempt to balance this tradeoff via posterior sampling or multi-stage generative pipelines, yet remain computationally expensive and architecturally complex. To overcome these limitations, we propose PCFlow (Perceptually Consistent Flow Matching), a unified framework that directly parameterizes a continuous transport from degraded observations to clean targets, jointly optimizing distortion and perceptual quality. While its latent consistency flow objective drives stable and efficient few-step inference, a Latent Consistency Perceptual Loss (LCPL) imposes semantic constraints directly on the guiding velocity field, steering the dynamics toward visually sharp data manifolds. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent conflict between structural and perceptual consistencies, we integrate a conflict-free gradient projection strategy to stabilize the multi-objective optimization landscape. Combined with lightweight, convolution-only backbone, PCFlow achieves competitive performance across diverse restoration tasks at a fraction of traditional computational costs.

</details>


### [43] [A HamNoSys-Guided Dataset and Baselines for Fine-Grained Isolated Handshape Recognition in Sign Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10588)
*Ushnish Sarkar,Suvajit Patra,Bhaswar Chattopadhyay,Pranab Singha Roy,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于HamNoSys手势符号系统、包含160个手形类别和144,000张RGB图像的大规模细粒度手形识别基准数据集，并系统地评估了四种模型族（ResNet-18、ViT-B/16、图卷积网络和XGBoost），同时引入了类分层受试者依赖分割和15折留一受试者（LOSO）两种评估协议，展示了模型在跨受试者泛化时的显著性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度手形识别对计算手语转写、识别和翻译至关重要，但现有的基于语音学定义的视觉手形库在不同手语者上的评估仍然有限，缺乏一个广泛且具有符号学依据、同时考虑手语者差异的基准。

Method: 从15名参与者收集了144,000张RGB图像，覆盖由官方HamNoSys 4手形图表定义的160个手形类别。外观模型采用ResNet-18和ViT-B/16，基于手部关键点的模型采用图卷积网络和XGBoost。评估采用类分层受试者依赖分割和15折LOSO协议，并在LSWH100和ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A上进行外部验证。

Result: 受试者依赖基准为四种模型族建立了可复现的参考性能；LOSO评估显示当需要泛化到未见参与者时识别性能显著下降。在ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A上，LOSO平均top-1准确率在82.20%到87.40%之间。

Conclusion: 本文提供的采集、整理和互补评估流程为细粒度孤立手形研究及更易获取的手语技术开发建立了可复现的资源基准。

Abstract: Purpose: Fine-grained handshape recognition supports computational sign-language transcription, recognition, and translation, but broad, phonetically defined visual inventories with signer-aware evaluation remain limited. This work introduces a benchmark grounded in the language-independent Hamburg Notation System (HamNoSys). Methods: A balanced dataset of 144,000 RGB images was collected from 15 participants for 160 handshape classes defined by the official HamNoSys 4 Handshapes Chart. ResNet-18 and ViT-B/16 were evaluated as appearance-based models, while a graph convolutional network and XGBoost were evaluated from hand landmarks. Both a class-stratified subject-dependent split and a 15-fold leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) protocol were used. The same model families were additionally assessed on LSWH100 and ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A for external context. Results: The subject-dependent benchmarks established reproducible reference performance across all four model families, whereas LOSO evaluation exposed a substantial reduction when recognition was required to generalise to unseen participants. On ASL Fingerspelling Dataset A, mean LOSO top-1 accuracy ranged from 82.20% to 87.40%. Conclusion: The documented acquisition, curation, and complementary evaluation protocols pro-vide a reproducible resource for fine-grained isolated-handshape research and for developing more accessible sign-language technologies.

</details>


### [44] [$π$-SUB: A Physics-Informed Synthetic Underwater Benchmark Dataset for Underwater Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10589)
*Namritha Lasyapriya Maddali,Rajini Makam,Suresh Sundaram,Narasimhan Sundararajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出π-SUB，一个基于物理信息的水下合成基准数据集生成框架，用于弥合水下图像增强（UIE）中合成数据与真实数据之间的差距。该框架扩展了经典水下图像形成模型，纳入深度相关下行辐照度、生物解析吸收和环境散射（覆盖全部十种Jerlov水类型），并支持独立可控的残余现象。通过广泛的仿真实验，π-SUB在超真实感（FID比Syrea低46%）和泛化能力（UIQM比PHISWID高4.18%、比Syrea高9.46%；NIQE分别降低48.78%和23.98%）两个维度上均显著优于现有合成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下图像增强（UIE）方法的训练数据大多来自合成数据集，但这些合成数据与真实水下场景之间存在显著的domain gap，导致模型在真实数据上的泛化能力不足。现有的合成数据集在物理建模的保真度和覆盖范围的广度上存在局限，亟需一种更具物理真实性和覆盖更广泛水下环境的合成数据生成框架。

Method: 提出物理信息驱动的π-SUB框架：(1) 扩展经典水下图像形成模型，加入深度相关的下行辐照度、生物解析的吸收与散射建模；(2) 覆盖全部十种Jerlov水类型，支持从浅海到深海、从近岸到远洋的环境多样性；(3) 提供独立可控的残余现象（如悬浮颗粒、光照波动等）以增强合成数据的真实性；(4) 生成成对的合成水下图像与参考图像，构建π-SUB数据集。

Result: 在超真实感评估中，π-SUB的全局FID比Syrea低46%；在泛化性评估中，使用四种SOTA UIE架构（FUnIE-GAN、Pix2Pix、PUIE-Net、Phaseformer）在六个数据集上训练并在六个真实基准数据集上测试，π-SUB相比PHISWID（次优）将UIQM提升4.18%、NIQE降低48.78%；相比Syrea将UIQM提升9.46%、NIQE降低23.98%。

Conclusion: π-SUB被验证为一种超真实且泛化能力强的水下合成基准数据集，可作为开发下一代水下图像增强方法的高质量训练资源。代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents $π$-SUB, a physics-informed framework for generating synthetic underwater benchmark datasets that bridges the synthetic-to-real gap for Underwater Image Enhancement (UIE). The proposed framework extends the classical underwater image formation model by incorporating depth-dependent downwelling irradiance, biologically resolved absorption, and environmental scattering across all ten Jerlov water types, together with independently controllable residual phenomena. Using this framework, the $π$-SUB dataset consists of paired synthetic underwater-reference images spanning shallow-to-deep and coastal-to-oceanic environments. Extensive simulation studies have been carried out to evaluate $π$-SUB along two criteria namely hyper-realism and generalizability. For hyper-realism, $π$-SUB attains a global Frechet Inception Distance (FID) that is 46% lower than Syrea. For generalizability, four state-of-the-art UIE architectures (FUnIE-GAN, Pix2Pix, PUIE-Net, and Phaseformer) are used for comparative evaluation of $π$-SUB. These models were independently trained on six datasets including one real and five synthetic datasets and tested on six real-world benchmarks datasets. Across four UIE architectures and six real benchmark datasets, $π$-SUB improves UIQM by 4.18% over PHISWID (next best) and 9.46% over Syrea (next best), while reducing NIQE by 48.78% and 23.98%, respectively. These results establish $π$-SUB as a hyper-realistic and generalizable benchmark for developing the next generation of underwater image enhancement methods. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/airl-iisc/pi-SUB

</details>


### [45] [Rethinking Data Efficiency in Industrial Dense Prediction: Pretraining Coherence, Not Inductive Bias, Determines ViTs Low-Data Advantage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10590)
*Haoran Sui,Yaoyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过四个工业数据集上的受控实验，发现Vision Transformer（ViT）在少样本工业密集预测中数据效率低于CNN的原因并非自注意力机制本身的缺陷，而是预训练不一致（pretraining incoherence）——即ImageNet预训练的ViT主干与COCO预训练的CNN颈部之间存在统计失配。作者提出轻量级AlignBlock系列进行金字塔级特征重校准，并发现数据效率边界：域近似场景≥200样本时，Swin-Graft超过YOLOv11x；域相远场景CNN仍占优；嫁接颈部权重比随机初始化颈部可提升至多2.5倍mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点普遍认为ViT在工业密集预测任务中需要比CNN更多的标注数据，但缺乏严格的受控实验来验证这一论断的真正来源。作者旨在探究这种"数据效率差距"的本质原因——究竟是自注意力机制固有的弱点，还是预训练过程中的某些设计缺陷。

Method: 作者在四个工业数据集上设计受控实验，对比ImageNet预训练的ViT主干与COCO预训练的CNN颈部组合，表征跨架构特征差距，并提出轻量级AlignBlock系列用于金字塔级特征重校准，以消除预训练不一致带来的特征失配。

Result: 实验揭示了数据效率边界：在域近似场景（≥200样本）下，Swin-Graft达到0.973 mAP@50，超越YOLOv11x的0.956；在域相远场景（141-shot）下，CNN仍具优势（0.900 vs 0.600 mAP@50）。使用嫁接颈部权重相比随机初始化颈部可带来最高2.5倍的mAP提升。

Conclusion: ViT在少样本工业密集预测中的数据效率低下并非源于自注意力架构本身，而是源于预训练不一致。通过特征重校准和颈部权重嫁接可有效弥合这一差距，且数据效率边界取决于场景与预训练域的接近程度。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are widely believed to require more labeled data than CNNs for industrial dense prediction. Through controlled experiments on four industrial datasets, we show that the data-efficiency gap stems from pretraining incoherence, which refers to the statistical mismatch between ImageNet-pretrained ViT backbones and COCO-pretrained CNN necks, rather than from inherent self-attention deficits. We characterize the cross-architecture feature gap and propose a lightweight AlignBlock family for pyramid-level feature recalibration. Our core finding empirically identifies a data-efficiency frontier: for domain-proximal scenes with >= 200 samples, Swin-Graft surpasses YOLOv11x (terminal 703-shot: 0.973 vs 0.956 mAP@50); for domain-distant scenes, CNNs retain advantage (hook 141-shot: 0.900 vs 0.600 mAP@50). Grafted neck weights yield up to 2.5x the mAP of a randomly initialized neck.

</details>


### [46] [Gaussian Sculpting: End-to-End Controllable Surface Reconstruction via Field Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10602)
*Ke Jiaxin,Juncheng Liu,Yi Wang,Zhouhui Lian,Bin Liu,Shengfa Wang,Xiangjia He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Gaussian Sculpting框架，将3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）锚定在可微分的演化表面上，通过双层训练策略（外层优化SDF几何、内层更新高斯参数）联合优化几何与外观，并引入基于八叉树的多分辨率细分方案，在少视角场景下实现高质量表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在少视角和天然不均匀的高斯基元下难以恢复精确表面，产生的几何错误难以人工修正。现有方法仅在后期处理中从高斯中提取表面，缺乏将高斯与几何优化联合进行的机制。

Method: 1) 将高斯锚定在演化的可微分表面上，引导SDF优化而非仅在后期提取表面；2) 设计双层（bi-level）训练策略：外层优化SDF几何，内层固定几何更新高斯参数，以实现稳定的梯度隔离；3) 对高斯参数施加约束确保其与底层表面一致；4) 提出基于八叉树划分的多分辨率细分方案，在保持细节的同时降低内存消耗。

Result: 在物体级场景上，该方法能有效去除冗余表面、恢复少视角下缺失的结构，在较低分辨率下也能实现高质量的重建。

Conclusion: Gaussian Sculpting通过端到端的可微分框架将高斯引导纳入SDF几何优化过程，显著提升了3DGS在少视角条件下的表面重建质量，实现了几何与外观的联合优化。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently enabled real-time novel view synthesis with impressive quality. However, it struggles to recover accurate surfaces under limited viewpoints and due to the inherent irregularity of Gaussian primitives. The resulting geometric errors are notoriously difficult to correct manually. To address these issues, we propose Gaussian Sculpting, a fully differentiable end-to-end framework for high-quality surface reconstruction. Our key insight is to anchor Gaussians onto an evolving differentiable surface, allowing them to guide signed distance field (SDF) optimization instead of extracting the surface only during post-processing. To enable stable gradient isolation during joint optimization, we design a bi-level training strategy in which the outer loop optimizes the geometry represented by the SDF, while the inner loop updates the Gaussians with the geometry fixed. We further impose constraints on Gaussian parameters to ensure consistency with the underlying surface, thereby improving both geometric and appearance fidelity during optimization. In addition, we introduce a multi-resolution subdivision scheme based on octree-like partitioning to preserve fine details while reducing memory consumption. Experiments on object-level scenes demonstrate that our method effectively removes redundant surfaces, recovers missing structures caused by limited viewpoints, and achieves strong reconstruction quality even at relatively low resolutions.

</details>


### [47] [InSight-doc: Agentic Visual Perception for Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10628)
*Kaican Li,Weiyan Xie,Lewei Yao,Jiannan Wu,Lanqing Hong,Yongxiang Huang,Nevin L. Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InSight-doc是一个智能体视觉感知框架，将视觉分辨率视为可自适应调整的推理时资源。它从低分辨率出发，通过SFT+RL训练，选择性放大高分辨率区域获取细粒度证据，无需外部检索器。在文档VQA基准上较基线提升4.3-16.4个准确率点，长文档场景下幻觉减少超40%，推理延迟降低41%-68%，同时保持准确率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档理解需要对大量视觉丰富页面进行推理，导致推理成本高昂且容易产生上下文腐烂（context rot）问题。现有方法在处理长文档视觉理解时效率低、准确性受限，缺乏一种能自适应分配计算资源的机制。

Method: 提出InSight-doc智能体视觉感知框架：1）从低分辨率开始，基于代理决策选择性放大到高分辨率区域以获取细粒度证据，无需外部检索器；2）构建包含17.9K高质量SFT示例（带区域级放大轨迹）和19.2K困难RL示例的主动感知语料库；3）采用SFT+RL两阶段训练范式训练8B参数的智能体模型。

Result: InSight-doc-8B在文档VQA基准上较基线提升4.3-16.4个准确率点；在长文档场景中，幻觉减少超过40%，推理延迟降低41%-68%，同时保持准确率领先优势。

Conclusion: 将视觉分辨率作为自适应推理时资源的思路是有效的，能够在提升长文档理解准确率的同时显著降低推理成本与幻觉，为视觉文档理解提供了一种高效且可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Long-document understanding often requires reasoning over many visually rich pages, making inference costly and prone to context rot. In this work, we propose InSight-doc, an agentic visual perception framework that treats visual resolution as an adaptive reasoning-time resource. InSight-doc starts from low resolution and selectively zooms into high-resolution regions for finer evidence, without relying on any external retriever. To train such an agent, we construct an active-perception corpus of 17.9K high-quality SFT examples with region-level zoom-in trajectories, accompanied by 19.2K hard RL examples. Through SFT+RL, InSight-doc-8B improves the baseline by 4.3--16.4 accuracy points over document VQA benchmarks. On long documents, it reduces hallucination by more than 40% and inference latency by 41%--68% while maintaining an accuracy lead. Our code, datasets, and model are released at https://github.com/m-Just/InSight-doc .

</details>


### [48] [MedUP: Awakening Unified Understanding and Perception in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10635)
*Yuan Wang,Hualiang Wang,Yixin Chen,Songtao Jiang,Shujian Gao,Jiaming Lin,Siming Fu,Jian Wu,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedUP是一种医学视觉语言模型，通过UniMedTok区域分词器将分割掩码编码为LLM词汇表中的离散token，在共享token空间中统一了感知与理解能力，并在多个医学视觉任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Med-VLM在精确视觉感知、分割和定位方面仍具挑战。已有方法要么将区域编码为坐标字符串，要么依赖外部模块将感知与理解解耦，导致区域与语言对齐存在表征鸿沟。这促使研究者探索一种原生统一感知与理解的方法。

Method: 提出MedUP模型，核心是UniMedTok区域分词器，将掩码编码为LLM词汇表中的离散token，使模型能够将掩码token与文本无缝交错。同时构建了UniMed-Train语料库（184万实例），涵盖文本引导分割、区域接地理解、医学VQA和基于思维链的分割，并引入UniMed-Bench进行统一评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，MedUP在所有任务上优于native、agentic和dual-decoder类Med-VLM，同时与专业分割器性能相当，展示了统一理解与感知建模的强大潜力。

Conclusion: 将感知（分割掩码token化）与理解（语言生成）原生统一在同一token空间中，是提升医学视觉语言模型区域级理解和分割能力的有效路径，为Med-VLM的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) excel at verbalizing visual content, yet precise visual perception, segmentation, and grounding remain challenging. Existing approaches either verbalize regions as coordinate strings or rely on external modules that decouple perception from understanding, creating representation gaps for region-language alignment. We present MedUP, a Med-VLM that natively unifies perception and understanding within a shared token space. At its core lies UniMedTok, a region tokenizer that encodes masks as discrete tokens in the LLM vocabulary, enabling the model to seamlessly interleave mask tokens with text. We curate UniMed-Train, a 1.84M-instance corpus spanning text-guided segmentation, region-grounded understanding, medical VQA and CoT-based segmentation, and introduce UniMed-Bench for unified evaluation. Extensive experiments show that MedUP outperforms native, agentic, and dual-decoder Med-VLMs across all tasks while remaining competitive with specialist segmentors, demonstrating the strong potential of unified understanding and perception modeling.

</details>


### [49] [PolypVision: A Three-Stage Hierarchical Deep Learning Framework for Classification and Segmentation of Colorectal Polyps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10649)
*Hamidreza Bolhasani,Hamidreza Rastad,Amir Mohammad Akbari,Mohammad Tashakoripour,Parnian Asadollahi,Ata Khodami,Mojgan Forootan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PolypVision是一个三阶段分层深度学习框架，用于结直肠息肉的检测、分割和分类。它依次完成（1）腺瘤/增生性息肉二分类及巴黎和JNet分类、（2）息肉分割及切除建议、（3）腺瘤亚型分类。在PolypGen、Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ClinicDB三个公开数据集上，帧分类AUC约0.99，检测mAP@50达94.4%，优于或持平现有最先进方法。框架与设备无关，可跨内镜成像系统使用，并已作为免费Web应用发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌是全球癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一，主要源于癌前息肉。准确检测、分割和内镜及组织学分类结直肠息肉对于临床及时干预至关重要，因此需要开发自动化、临床可用的分析工具。

Method: 采用三阶段分层深度学习框架：（1）使用EfficientNetV2-M和Focal Loss进行腺瘤/增生性二分类及巴黎和JNet分类；（2）以第1阶段骨干网络作为编码器，使用UNet++解码器，以Dice和BCE损失优化进行息肉分割；（3）通过第2阶段的迁移学习，使用EfficientNetV2-M进行腺瘤亚型分类。使用Grad-CAM验证模型关注的临床相关病变特征。

Result: 在PolypGen、Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ClinicDB三个公开数据集上，帧分类AUC约0.99，Kvasir-SEG检测mAP@50达94.4%，优于或匹配现有最先进方法。Grad-CAM确认模型关注临床相关的病变特征，且框架具备设备无关性。

Conclusion: 分层、迁移学习驱动的流水线结合任务特异性损失函数，为自动化结直肠息肉分析提供了稳健、设备无关且临床有意义的方法。PolypVision作为免费Web应用公开提供给所有用户使用。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer (CRC) remains one of the leading causes of cancer-related mortality worldwide, predominantly arising from precancerous polyps. Accurate detection, segmentation, and endoscopic and histological classification of colorectal polyps are crucial for timely clinical intervention. In this study, we present PolypVision, a three-stage hierarchical deep learning framework that sequentially performs: (Stage 1) binary classification of polyps as adenomatous or hyperplastic, with simultaneous Paris and JNet classification, using EfficientNetV2-M with Focal Loss; (Stage 2) polyp segmentation with recommended resection method using a UNet++ decoder with the Stage 1 backbone as encoder, optimized with Dice and BCE losses; and (Stage 3) adenoma subtype classification (tubular, tubulovillous, villous) using EfficientNetV2-M with transfer learning from Stage 2. Evaluated on three public datasets -- PolypGen, Kvasir-SEG, and CVC-ClinicDB -- PolypVision achieves an AUC of approximately 0.99 for frame classification and a detection mAP@50 of 94.4% on Kvasir-SEG, outperforming or matching state-of-the-art methods. Gradient-weighted Class Activation Maps (Grad-CAM) confirm that the model attends to clinically relevant lesion features. The framework is device-independent, operating across diverse endoscopic imaging systems without hardware-specific adaptation. These results demonstrate that a hierarchical, transfer-learning-driven pipeline with task-specific loss functions offers a robust, device-independent, and clinically meaningful approach to automated colorectal polyp analysis. PolypVision is freely available as a web application at https://polypvision.com, a DataBioX initiative, with a free usage tier open to all users.

</details>


### [50] [Cross-View Sequential Visual Localization with Spatio-Temporal Context Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10660)
*Jiaping Wang,Shaobo Li,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时间上下文增强的跨视角序列视觉定位框架。通过循环跨帧模块聚合历史帧信息增强当前帧地面特征，结合卫星候选区域分类和层次化细粒度特征实现精确偏移估计。在CVIS数据集上将平均定位误差从3.80m降至1.57m，R@1m从8.14%提升至40.22%，并验证了在KITTI-CVL及真实道路上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角视觉定位方法大多逐帧独立处理，未能充分利用时序信息，导致在动态遮挡、光照变化和重复纹理场景下精度受限。为克服这一局限，作者提出利用时间上下文增强定位性能。

Method: 提出时间上下文增强框架：1）循环跨帧模块（recurrent cross-frame module）从先验状态聚合历史上下文以增强当前帧的粗粒度地面特征；2）增强特征用于卫星候选区域分类；3）层次化细粒度特征实现局部偏移的精确估计。

Result: 在CVIS数据集上平均定位误差从3.80m降至1.57m，R@1m从8.14%提升至40.22%；直接迁移至KITTI-CVL平均误差2.61m，目标域微调后降至2.27m；真实车辆零样本实验中平均误差2.84m，R@5m达96.86%。

Conclusion: 时间上下文增强显著提升跨视角定位精度，方法在公开基准和真实道路场景中均表现出良好的鲁棒性与部署可行性。

Abstract: Continuous and reliable localization is essential for autonomous driving. Cross-view visual localization matches ground images with satellite maps, providing complementary localization cues for pipelines that depend on Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals and high-definition (HD) maps. Most existing cross-view visual localization methods process each frame independently, leaving temporal information underused and limiting accuracy under dynamic occlusion, illumination variation, and repetitive textures. This study proposes a temporal-context-enhanced framework for cross-view sequence visual localization. The proposed recurrent cross-frame module aggregates historical context from the previous state to enhance the coarse ground feature of each current frame. These enhanced features facilitate satellite candidate-region classification, while hierarchical fine-grained features enable precise local offset estimation. On the CVIS dataset, the proposed method reduces mean localization error from 3.80 m to 1.57 m and increases R@1 m from 8.14% to 40.22%. Direct transfer to KITTI-CVL achieves a mean error of 2.61 m, with target-domain fine-tuning further reducing the mean error to 2.27 m. Zero-shot field experiments on a real-world vehicle achieve a mean error of 2.84 m and R@5 m of 96.86%. These results demonstrate that temporal context enhancement significantly improves cross-view localization accuracy and supports robust deployment on public benchmarks and real-world roads.

</details>


### [51] [Chartography: A Benchmark for Professional Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10677)
*Suhaas Garre,Chris Mutty,Sushant Mehta,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Chartography 是一个包含100个任务的基准测试，使用专业领域图表和专家编写的问题来评估AI模型阅读专业图表的能力。在30个前沿模型配置（每任务20次评分试验）中，最佳配置仅达到45.0%的pass@1均值，其余配置在9.0-39.5%之间。失败主要集中在视觉感知方面，包括细微特征遗漏、稀疏标注轴上的数值误读、3D投影几何处理错误以及违反图表编码的领域约定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表基准测试存在三个主要缺陷：格式过于集中在条形图、折线图和饼图；推理链条较短；已被前沿模型（80-90%准确率）接近饱和。然而专业人士在医学、工程、金融、制造和科学领域经常需要从图表中做出关键决策，因此需要更严格、更真实的图表理解基准。

Method: 作者构建Chartography基准，包含100个任务，每个任务对来自专业实践中的专业领域格式图表（标准图表基准很少包含）配对由日常阅读这些图表的专业人士编写的问题，并由另外三位专家独立验证。评估使用30个前沿模型配置，每个任务进行20次评分试验。

Result: 最佳配置达到45.0%的mean pass@1，其余配置在9.0-39.5%范围内。失败集中于视觉感知：模型无法捕捉细微特征、在稀疏标注轴上误读数值、处理投影3D几何时有误，以及违反图表中编码的领域约定。

Conclusion: 该基准揭示了前沿模型在专业图表阅读上的显著不足，表明现有模型能力被标准图表基准高估。作者发布了所有任务、图像、来源元数据和评估代码，以支持该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Professionals across medicine, engineering, finance, manufacturing, and the sciences often make consequential decisions from charts. Existing chart benchmarks do not sufficiently measure this ability: they are dominated by bar, line, and pie formats, rely on shorter reasoning chains, and are nearing saturation, with frontier models already scoring 80-90%. We introduce Chartography, a benchmark of 100 tasks that pair charts drawn from professional practice, in domain-specific formats that standard chart benchmarks rarely include, with questions written by professionals who read these charts for a living and independently verified by three additional experts. In an evaluation of 30 frontier-model configurations (20 scored trials per task), the best configuration reaches only 45.0% mean pass@1; the remainder span 9.0-39.5%. Failures concentrate in visual perception: models can miss nuanced features, misread values along sparsely labeled axes, mishandle projected 3D geometry, and violate domain conventions encoded in the chart. We release all tasks, images, provenance metadata, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [52] [Bridging Severe Cross-Modal Misalignment: End-to-End Visible-Infrared Object Detection via Explicit Feature-Domain Affine Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10680)
*Qi Ming,Yuyang Wang,Mingjing Zhao,Yifan Xiao,Zhixin Guo,Zhiqiang Zhou,Peng Sun,Juan Fang,Fuqiang Yang,Xudong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出JFRDet网络，一个端到端的可见光-红外定向目标检测器，专门解决严重跨模态几何错位问题。通过Cross-Modal Affine Alignment模块显式估计图像级仿射变换进行多级特征对齐，Illumination-Guided Complementary Fusion模块根据光照条件自适应融合模态特征，Alignment Quality-Consistency Gating策略稳定联合优化。同时构建了DVMA基准数据集，在严重跨模态几何错位场景下达到69.7% mAP50的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可见光-红外目标检测方法多依赖隐式特征适应处理弱错位场景，对大幅偏移的几何差异处理不足，导致检测性能严重下降。

Method: JFRDet包含三个核心模块：(1) Cross-Modal Affine Alignment (CMAA)显式估计图像级仿射变换，实现多层级特征对齐；(2) Illumination-Guided Complementary Fusion (IGCF)根据光照条件自适应评估模态可靠性进行融合；(3) Alignment Quality-Consistency Gating (AQCG)通过对齐可靠性和梯度一致性调控检测监督，稳定联合优化。此外构建了DVMA基准数据集。

Result: 在DVMA数据集上达到69.7% mAP50，实现SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 显式仿射对齐与光照感知融合相结合，可以有效解决严重跨模态几何错位下的可见光-红外定向目标检测问题，代码和数据集将开源。

Abstract: Visible-infrared object detection relies on complementary RGB and thermal cues, but its performance is often degraded by cross-modal spatial misalignment. Most existing methods rely on implicit feature adaptation to handle weakly misaligned scenarios, while large-offset geometric discrepancies remain insufficiently addressed. In this paper, we propose a Joint Feature-domain Registration and Detection network (JFRDet), an end-to-end visible-infrared oriented object detector tailored for severely cross-modal geometric discrepancies. JFRDet introduces a Cross-Modal Affine Alignment (CMAA) module to estimate an image-level affine transformation for explicit multi-level feature alignment. Note that illumination changes directly affect the reliability of RGB cues, an Illumination-Guided Complementary Fusion (IGCF) module adaptively exploits modality reliability under varying illumination conditions for cross-modal fusion. Then, an Alignment Quality-Consistency Gating (AQCG) strategy stabilizes joint optimization by modulating detection supervision according to alignment reliability and gradient consistency. We further construct DroneVehicle Misaligned (DVMA), a benchmark for evaluating visible-infrared oriented object detection under severe cross-modal geometric misalignment. The proposed JFRDet achieves 69.7\% $\mathrm{mAP}_{50}$ on DVMA, which represents state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance. The code and dataset will be available on GitHub.

</details>


### [53] [Visual Geometry Foundation-Aware Gaussians for Single-Frame Surround-View Driving Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10682)
*Junhong Lin,Jinlong Wang,Xianda Guo,Yanlun Peng,Wei Zheng,Guoqing Liu,Hanli Wang,Tiesong Zhao,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGGD框架，利用预训练视觉几何先验(VGGT)来增强上游特征表示，通过Dual-Path Neck解耦几何与外观表征、Scale Warmup稳定早期几何学习，以及混合像素-体积高斯解码器，在单帧环视驾驶场景重建中实现最佳渲染质量和改善的几何一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单帧环视重建面临严重的几何不稳定性和渲染伪影，根源在于相机间重叠区域极小导致稀疏视角下几何歧义大。现有方法虽采用复杂解码器或辅助线索，但仍受限于上游特征的弱几何表达能力。

Method: 1) 利用VGGT提供可迁移的多视角几何先验Token；2) 引入Dual-Path Neck解耦几何一致性与外观感知表征，改善弱观测区域的外观补全；3) 应用Scale Warmup稳定早期几何学习并抑制自车姿态变化下的尺度漂移；4) 采用混合像素-体积高斯解码器生成可渲染的3D高斯场景。

Result: 在nuScenes单帧基准上，VGGD在所有对比方法中取得最佳的整体渲染质量，并提升了相对几何一致性。

Conclusion: 将几何建模前移至前端、利用预训练视觉几何先验增强上游表示，能有效缓解稀疏环视视角下的几何歧义问题，为前馈式环绕驾驶重建提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Single-frame surround-view reconstruction faces severe geometric instability and rendering artifacts due to minimal inter-camera overlap. While existing methods rely on complex decoders or auxiliary cues, they remain bottlenecked by the weak geometric capacity of upstream features. We argue that leveraging pretrained visual geometry priors strengthens upstream representations and alleviates the geometric ambiguity in sparse surround views. To this end, we propose VGGD, a visual geometry foundation-aware 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for feed-forward surround-view driving reconstruction, which shifts geometric modeling to the frontend and adapts foundation priors to the driving camera setting. First, VGGD leverages VGGT to provide transferable multi-view geometric prior tokens. Next, we introduce a Dual-Path Neck to decouple geometry-consistent and appearance-aware representations, improving appearance completion in weakly observed regions. We further apply Scale Warmup to stabilize early geometry learning and suppress scale drift under ego-pose changes. Finally, we use a hybrid pixel--volume Gaussian decoder to produce a renderable 3D Gaussian scene for novel-view synthesis. Experiments on the nuScenes single-frame benchmark show that VGGD achieves the best overall rendering quality among the compared methods and improves relative geometric consistency.

</details>


### [54] [Embedding Rotation Invariance for Provable Multi-Oriented Scene Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10684)
*Zhibin Ma,Pengwen Dai,Yi Liu,Xugong Qin,Chenyun Yu,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RISTER，一个端到端全旋转不变的场景文本识别网络，通过在编码器中嵌入旋转等变卷积与自注意力、在解码器中利用并证明交叉注意力的旋转不变性，实现具有理论保证的旋转不变STR，无需额外推理计算或数据驱动方向校正，在多方向基准上超越第二名4.0%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方向文本在自然场景中普遍存在，是场景文本识别（STR）的主要挑战。现有基于旋转感知的方法需显式估计文本方向，但由于缺乏理论保证，容易产生误差累积、计算开销增加以及对数据集的强依赖等问题。作者希望从根本上解决这些问题，即通过将旋转不变性直接嵌入STR框架，而非依赖显式的方向估计。

Method: 采用编码器-解码器架构：(1) 编码器端提出旋转等变局部-全局提取网络（rotation-equivariant local-global extraction network），将深度等变卷积与自注意力结合，实现旋转等变的特征提取，同时建模字符间依赖并保留细粒度视觉细节；(2) 解码器端首先识别并证明了交叉注意力机制的旋转不变性，据此构建旋转不变文本解码器，以旋转不变方式将视觉特征映射为输出文本。编码器与解码器集成后构成端到端RISTER网络，提供具有理论保证的旋转不变性。

Result: 实验表明RISTER在标准和多方向基准上均达到最先进性能，在通用多方向数据集上准确率超越第二名模型4.0个百分点，且无需额外推理计算，也不依赖数据驱动的方向校正。

Conclusion: 通过在编码器和解码器中分别嵌入旋转等变性和旋转不变性，RISTER实现了具有理论保证的端到端旋转不变STR，有效解决现有方法误差累积、计算开销和数据依赖的问题，在多方向文本识别任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Multi-oriented text is ubiquitous in real-world scenes and remains a major challenge for scene text recognition (STR). Existing rotation-aware methods explicitly estimate text orientation. However, due to the lack of theoretical guarantees, they are prone to error accumulation, increased computational cost, and strong reliance on data. In this work, we incorporate rotation invariance into the STR framework to address these limitations. Specifically, we adopt an encoder-decoder architecture, embedding rotation equivariance in the encoder and rotation invariance in the decoder to construct a fully rotation-invariant network. On the decoder side, we first identify and prove the rotation-invariant property of the cross-attention mechanism and use it to formulate a rotation-invariant text decoder that maps visual features to output text in a rotation-invariant manner. On the encoder side, we propose a rotation-equivariant local-global extraction network that integrates deep equivariant convolutions with self-attention, enabling rotation-equivariant feature extraction while modeling inter-character dependencies and preserving fine-grained visual details. By integrating the encoder and decoder, we obtain an end-to-end Rotation-Invariant Scene Text Recognition network (RISTER). RISTER provides rotation invariance with theoretical guarantees, enhancing robustness on multi-oriented samples without introducing additional inference computation or relying on data-driven orientation correction. Experiments show that RISTER achieves state-of-the-art performance on both standard and multi-oriented benchmarks, surpassing the second-best model by 4.0 percent in accuracy on the general multi-oriented dataset.

</details>


### [55] [MMArt A Multi-Perspective Multimodal Dataset for Visual Art Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10706)
*Shuai Wang,Wangyuan Ding,Yixian Shen,Jia-Hong Huang,Stevan Rudinac,Monika Kackovic,Nachoem Wijnberg,Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMArt，一个包含74,234幅WikiArt画作的大规模多视角艺术数据集，每幅作品同时标注了叙事、形式、情感和历史四种独立视角以及一个统合的统一标题。通过生成性和判别性两种互补性分析证明这四种视角编码了真正不同的信息，且没有任何单一视角能胜任所有任务，从而验证了多视角设计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型对艺术的理解仍然浅显，只描述表面内容而缺乏形式分析、历史解释和情感刻画。作者认为这不仅是模型局限也是数据集局限——现有艺术数据集都是单一视角资源，没有任何数据集能同时为同一批艺术作品提供叙事、形式、情感和历史视角的标注。

Method: 构建MMArt数据集：从WikiArt收集74,234幅画作，每幅由专门的视觉语言模型或人工标注生成四个独立标注视角（叙事、形式、情感、历史）加上一个统一化标题，并通过互补性质量评估验证。随后进行两种互补性分析：(1)生成性分析——检验每种描述在重建图像时对构图风格和情感信号的保真度；(2)判别性检索分析——检验每种描述在不同任务中的判别力；以及留一法分析检验各视角的可替代性。

Result: 生成性分析表明形式分析描述最能保留构图风格，历史描述在重建图像中承载强情感信号。判别性检索分析揭示了任务不对称性：叙事描述驱动检索（R@1=44.0%），而形式描述虽在重建中最强，但在检索尺度上几乎无判别力（R@1=7.8%）。留一分析进一步确证历史描述是两种任务中最不可替代的视角。

Conclusion: 两种互补性分析共同表明没有任何单一视角能同时满足所有任务的需求，这直接佐证了MMArt多视角设计方案的合理性。数据集、代码和附加信息已公开提供。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models demonstrate impressive general visual understanding, yet their art interpretation remains shallow: they describe surface content but struggle with formal analysis, grounded historical interpretation, or affective characterization. We argue this is not only a model but also a dataset limitation. Existing art datasets are single perspective resources, where no dataset provides narrative, formal, emotional, and historical perspectives simultaneously for the same artworks. We introduce MMArt, a large-scale dataset of 74,234 WikiArt paintings, each annotated with four independently annotated perspectives plus a harmonized unified caption, produced by specialized vision-language models or human annotation and validated through complementary quality evaluations. Two complementarity analyses establish that perspectives encode genuinely distinct information. A generative analysis shows that formal analysis descriptions best preserve compositional style, and historical descriptions carry strong affective signal in reconstructed images. A discriminative retrieval analysis reveals task-asymmetry: narrative descriptions drive retrieval (R@1 = 44.0%), while formal descriptions, strongest for reconstruction, are nearly nondiscriminative at retrieval scale (R@1 = 7.8%). Leave-one-out analysis further confirms that historical descriptions are the least replaceable perspective across both tasks. Together, the two analyses establish that no single perspective suffices for all tasks, directly motivating MMArt multi-perspective design. The dataset, code, and additional information are available at https://shuaiwang97.github.io/MMArt/.

</details>


### [56] [Self-Geometry: GT-Free and Plug-and-Play Test-Time Adaptation for Geometrically Consistent 3D Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10708)
*Seokhyun Youn,Dahyeon Kye,Sung-Ho Bae,Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-Geometry，一种即插即用的测试时自适应（TTA）管道，通过显式多视图几何约束（以2D像素对应作为伪真值）来提升视觉基础模型（VFM）在深度、相机位姿和点图估计中的几何一致性，在六个VFM和四个基准上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VFM虽然能单次前向推理预测深度、位姿和点图且泛化能力强，但预训练时未施加显式多视图几何一致性约束（如束调整），导致推理时可能存在不一致。此前依赖隐式自一致性（来自模型输出的点图、特征）的方法在VFM本身预测不准确的场景中性能提升有限，因此需要直接施加显式多视图几何约束。

Method: Self-Geometry包含三个核心组件：1) Geometric Disentanglement Optimization——结合多视图一致性损失和极线一致性损失，并采用梯度解耦（Gradient Disentanglement）防止梯度冲突；2) Frame Angular-Neighbor——基于SO(3)测地距离的视图采样器，以轻量方式施加约束；3) Lightweight TTA——通过LoRA对VFM进行参数高效微调适配。

Result: 在六个VFM（VGGT、π³、DA3-Giant/Large/Base/Small）和四个基准（7Scenes、ETH3D、ScanNet++、HiRoom）上，Self-Geometry在相机位姿和几何估计方面均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 相比基于隐式信号的测试时方法，直接施加显式多视图几何约束的测试时自适应能带来更有效的性能增益，且Self-Geometry作为一个即插即用模块可广泛适用于不同规模和类型的VFM。

Abstract: Recent Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) predict depth, camera pose, and pointmap in a single forward pass without per-scene optimization, achieving strong generalization. However, enforcing explicit multi-view geometric consistency, e.g., through bundle adjustment, is computationally costly and is thus not imposed during VFM pretraining, so such inconsistency can arise. To address this, implicit self-consistency derived from model outputs (e.g., pointmaps, features), though enforced at test-time in prior work, delivers inherently limited performance gain, especially on scenes where the pretrained VFM is highly inaccurate. In contrast to this implicit signal, we propose Self-Geometry, a plug-and-play test-time adaptation pipeline that directly imposes explicit multi-view geometric constraints using 2D pixel correspondences as pseudo ground-truth. Our proposed Self-Geometry consists of Geometric Disentanglement Optimization, which combines Multi-View Consistency and Epipolar Consistency losses with Gradient Disentanglement to prevent gradient conflict; Frame Angular-Neighbor, a view sampler based on SO(3) geodesic distances for lightly imposing these constraints; and Lightweight TTA, which adapts VFMs via LoRA. Our method achieves consistent improvements in both pose and geometry estimation across six VFMs (VGGT, $π^3$, DA3-Giant/Large/Base/Small) and four benchmarks (7Scenes, ETH3D, ScanNet++, HiRoom).

</details>


### [57] [Compact Feed-Forward 3D Gaussians via Saliency-Guided Primitive Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10712)
*Tim-Felix Fassch,Jochen Kall,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结构感知的合并流水线，将任意前馈3D高斯溅射方法生成的逐像素高斯原语整合为紧凑、内容自适应的3D高斯集合，仅用1/20的高斯数量保持大部分视觉质量，实现高效的渲染与存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D高斯溅射方法虽能从稀疏视角快速重建，但生成的逐像素高斯原语高度冗余、效率低下，需要一种能在保持视觉质量的同时大幅减少高斯数量的方法。

Method: 1) 通过自适应超像素分割（结合显著图引导）将空间邻近且外观相似的高斯分组为变尺寸簇；2) 用学习型编码器将每个簇压缩为紧凑潜表示；3) 基于几何重叠和特征相似性，通过学习型合并器跨视角匹配并整合表示；4) 用具有细节层级（LOD）的解码器在可控分辨率下生成最终高斯，支持推理时的质量-效率权衡。

Result: 该流水线将高斯数量减少至逐像素方法的1/20，同时保留大部分视觉质量，且相比先前降低原语数量的方法，实现了更好、更稳健的质量，并支持高效的渲染。

Conclusion: 该方法是骨干网络无关的后处理模块，可兼容任意现有前馈方法，提供高度紧凑且渲染高效的3D高斯表示，实现了分辨率和质量之间的灵活权衡。

Abstract: 3D scene reconstruction, modeling, and rendering are highly relevant for numerous tasks, and 3D Gaussian splatting has become a standard choice in this context. Its feed-forward variants provide fast reconstruction from sparse input views but often produce per-pixel primitives, leading to highly redundant and thus inefficient representations. We present a structure-aware merging pipeline that takes per-pixel primitives from any feed-forward method and consolidates them into a compact, content-adaptive Gaussian set while largely retaining visual quality at just $\frac{1}{20}^\text{th}$ of the Gaussians of a per-pixel method. We group spatially coherent Gaussians of similar appearance into variable-size clusters via adaptive superpixel segmentation guided by a saliency map, which allocates fine segments to textured regions and coarse segments to homogeneous areas. We compress each cluster into a compact latent representation through a learned encoder, then match and consolidate representations across views based on geometric overlap and feature similarity via a learned merger. A level-of-detail decoder then produces the final Gaussians at a controllable resolution, enabling a flexible quality-efficiency trade-off at inference. As a post-processing module, the pipeline is backbone-agnostic, leveraging the strengths of existing feed-forward methods. This leads to better and more robust quality than achieved by previous approaches that target a reduction in primitive count, while providing a highly compact representation, that can be rendered efficiently.

</details>


### [58] [Grid-Preserving Knowledge Distillation: Transferring Convolutional Inductive Bias to Vision Transformers under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10723)
*Junyong Choi,Cheolhyeon Park,Jaehoon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出iBKD框架，通过保留空间网格结构将CNN的归纳偏置蒸馏到Vision Transformer中，核心是Inductive Bias Attention Module，在训练时使用、推理零开销，在多个数据稀缺基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformer在训练数据稀缺时性能不及卷积网络，而现有的通用特征蒸馏方法忽略了空间网格结构，因为其在池化、展平和对数空间投影中丢弃了局部性和平移等变性编码的空间网格，ViT学生无法自行重建该结构。

Method: 提出iBKD蒸馏框架，其核心是Inductive Bias Attention Module：用学习权重将每个学生层聚合到教师网格上，通过通道和可变形空间注意力强化结构线索，再通过卷积交叉注意力在网格之间（而非token集合之间）注入这些线索。该模块仅在训练阶段使用。

Result: 在七个Transformer骨干网络和六个数据稀缺基准上，iBKD优于局部性引导方法和通用知识蒸馏基线，且随着训练数据减少，其优势进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 通过在完整传输路径上保留空间网格，iBKD有效将CNN归纳偏置迁移至ViT，且不增加部署模型推理开销，为数据稀缺场景下的ViT训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers underperform convolutional networks when training data is scarce, and distilling convolutional inductive biases from a CNN teacher is an effective remedy that leaves the deployed model unchanged. General-purpose feature distillation, however, transfers little in this setting. The pooling, flattening, and logit-space projections it inherits from CNN to CNN pipelines discard the spatial grid in which locality and translation equivariance are encoded, and unlike a convolutional student, a ViT cannot rebuild that structure on its own. In this paper, we propose iBKD, a distillation framework that preserves the grid along the entire transfer path. Its core module, the Inductive Bias Attention Module, aggregates every student layer onto the teacher grid with learned weights, sharpens structural cues with channel and deformable spatial attention, and injects them through convolutional cross-attention that operates between grids rather than between token sets. The module is used only during training, so the deployed model is an unmodified ViT with no inference overhead. Across seven Transformer backbones and six data-scarce benchmarks, iBKD outperforms both locality-guidance methods and general knowledge distillation baselines, and its margin widens as training data shrinks.

</details>


### [59] [InterPruner: Interactive Structured Pruning via Taylor-Implicit Criterion and Language-Prior Modulator for Multimodal Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10724)
*Qi Ming,Zihan Yang,Shaoguang Huang,Si Sun,Hanqing Zhang,Nanqing Liu,Jiahui Lv,Juan Fang,Aleksandra Pizurica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterPruner是首个面向RGB-红外目标检测器的交互式结构化通道剪枝框架，通过Taylor-Implicit准则、模态交互冗余分析器和场景先验通道锚点三个模块实现跨模态通道剪枝，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-红外多模态目标检测器虽然性能优越，但并行特征提取器带来大量通道冗余和计算开销。已有剪枝方法针对单模态骨干网络设计，忽略了跨模态交互和动态场景级冗余，亟需专门针对RGB-红外检测器的交互式剪枝方案。

Method: 提出InterPruner框架，包含三个核心组件：1) Taylor-Implicit Criterion (TIC)通过高阶泰勒展开和隐函数定理量化通道重要性；2) Modality Interaction Redundancy Analyzer (MIRA)通过互补偿性评估识别冗余通道；3) Scene-Prior Channel Anchor (SPCA)利用语言先验作为语义锚点衡量通道-场景相关性，实现动态通道重要性估计。

Result: 在RGB-红外目标检测数据集上的大量实验表明，InterPruner在保持高性能的同时仅有可忽略的性能衰减。特别是在FLIR数据集上，剪枝50%通道时甚至实现了0.6% mAP的提升。

Conclusion: InterPruner填补了RGB-红外检测中跨模态通道剪枝的研究空白，证明了交互式剪枝能够在显著降低计算冗余的同时维持甚至提升检测性能，代码将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Multimodal object detection proves effective in remote sensing, especially the RGB-Infrared paradigm. The parallel feature extractors provide rich multimodal information for robust detection, yet introduce substantial channel redundancy and computational overhead. Existing pruning methods can reduce channel redundancy, but they are designed for unimodal backbones, overlooking cross-modal interactions and dynamic scene-wise redundancy. In this paper, we propose InterPruner, the first interactive structured channel pruning framework for RGB-infrared object detectors. Specifically, we first derive a Taylor-Implicit Criterion(TIC) to quantify channel importance via high-order Taylor expansion and the implicit function theorem. Then, a Modality Interaction Redundancy Analyzer (MIRA) identifies redundant channels via mutual compensability assessment. Finally, a Scene-Prior Channel Anchor (SPCA) uses language priors as semantic anchors to measure channel-scene relevance for dynamic channel importance estimation. Cross-modality channel pruning for RGB-Infrared detection is yet unexplored. Extensive experiments on RGB-infrared object detection dataset demonstrate that InterPruner maintains high performance with negligible degradation. Specifically, it even achieves a 0.6% mAP increase on the FLIR dataset when pruning 50% of the channels. Code will be available on GitHub to facilitate future work.

</details>


### [60] [Rethinking LLM Verification: Evidence Structure, Uncertainty, and Selective Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10725)
*Uma Ranjan,Kunal Tilaganji,Aditya Koul,Anurag Mahipal,Dashpreet Singh,Hriday Rana,Manan Jain,Sidharth Gupta,Ajo Babu George,Vineeth Balasubramanian,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架用于医学假设验证，通过在模型弃权时才进行目标本体接地来管理覆盖-准确率权衡，将GPT-5.5和DeepSeek-R1的问题级准确率从82.9%提升至92.5%，假设级准确率从92.0%提升至96.2%，实现了无需显式知识图谱构建即可达到知识图谱级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在医学应用中常依赖捷径而非系统性推理，存在安全隐患。让模型在不确定时选择弃权可以提高可靠性，但引入了覆盖-准确率权衡问题。作者希望找到一种方法在保持高覆盖率的同时提升准确率。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架用于医学假设验证的多选设置。核心思想是：仅当模型弃权时才应用目标本体接地（ontology grounding），以此管理覆盖-准确率权衡。该框架将弃权视为不确定性信号，并将其重新用作选择性推理优化的控制信号。

Result: 在GPT-5.5和DeepSeek-R1两个前沿模型上，该框架将问题级准确率提升了9.6个百分点（82.9%到92.5%），假设级准确率提升了4.2个百分点（92.0%到96.2%）。实验在MedReason和MedQA数据集上进行，证明弃权不仅不是随机的，而是真实不确定性的反映（弃权预测对应较低置信度）。

Conclusion: 弃权可以被重新用作选择性推理优化的控制信号，该框架在不进行显式知识图谱构建的情况下达到了知识图谱级别的性能，有效解决了医学LLM应用中的覆盖-准确率权衡问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often rely on shortcuts rather than systematic reasoning, raising safety concerns in medical applications. Allowing models to abstain when uncertain improves reliability but introduces a coverage accuracy tradeoff. We propose a two-stage framework for medical hypothesis verification in multiple-choice settings that manages this tradeoff through targeted ontology grounding, applied only when the model abstains. We show that abstention is not random but reflects genuine uncertainty, with abstained predictions associated with lower confidence. Across two frontier models (GPT-5.5, accessed via the Azure OpenAI API, and DeepSeek-R1), the proposed framework improves question-level accuracy by 9.6 percentage points (82.9% to 92.5%) and hypothesis-level accuracy by 4.2 percentage points (92.0% to 96.2%). Our experiments conducted on MedReason and MedQA show that abstention can be repurposed as a control signal for selective reasoning refinement, achieving knowledge-graph-level performance without explicit knowledge graph construction.

</details>


### [61] [Beyond Pixels: From Video Priors to 4D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10744)
*Zihao Liu,Xiaolong Shen,Zhenglin Zhou,Ruijie Quan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Latent-to-4D 方法，直接利用共享 VAE 的视频模型去噪后潜变量作为输入，绕过 RGB 中间表示，将视频潜变量与预训练 4D 解码器的 token 网格对齐并细化，实现显式 4D 预测。仅用约 1K 重建片段训练，单个 checkpoint 可在同一 VAE 家族内跨多个视频扩散 Transformer 泛化，并在多个基准上超越级联方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 生成方法存在两类问题：一是用独立 4D 模型重建生成的 RGB 视频，存在分布不匹配与误差传播；二是将特定视频生成器直接改造为几何预测器，导致 4D 预测与特定生成器绑定，替换生成器或改变条件方式时需重新训练。因此需要一种解耦生成器与 4D 预测的通用接口。

Method: 核心洞察是共享 VAE 的视频模型最终去噪后潜变量可作为可复用的 4D 预测接口。Latent-to-4D 将视频潜变量与预训练 4D 解码器的 token 网格对齐，并通过帧级与全局时空注意力进行细化，绕过 RGB 中间表示，直接进行潜变量到 4D 的生成。

Result: 仅用约 1K 现有重建片段训练，单个 checkpoint 无需修改即可在同一 VAE 家族内的多个视频扩散 Transformer 上迁移。在 Text4D-200 和 I4D-200 基准上，相比匹配的同潜变量 Wan+4RC 级联方案，投影类 DINO-F1 指标分别高出 2.88-3.45 和 5.81 个点；人工评估在几何、时间稳定性和整体质量上也更受偏好。

Conclusion: 通过利用共享 VAE 的潜变量作为统一接口，成功解耦了视频生成与 4D 预测，实现了跨生成器的可迁移 4D 生成，同时提升了重建质量与泛化能力，避免了级联方法的分布不匹配和误差传播问题。

Abstract: 4D generation synthesizes dynamic 3D scenes from conditions such as text or images. Existing methods either reconstruct generated RGB videos with a separate 4D model or adapt a particular video generator to predict geometry directly. The former suffers from distribution mismatch and error propagation, whereas the latter ties 4D prediction to a specific generator and may require retraining when the generator or conditioning regime changes. We ask whether the final denoised latents of video models that share a variational autoencoder (VAE) can instead provide a reusable interface to explicit 4D prediction. Building on this insight, we introduce direct latent-to-4D generation and instantiate it as Latent-to-4D, which bypasses RGB by aligning a video latent with the token grid of a pretrained 4D decoder and refining it through frame-wise and global spatiotemporal attention. Trained on roughly 1K existing reconstruction clips, a single checkpoint transfers unchanged across multiple video diffusion transformers within the same VAE family. On Text4D-200 and I4D-200, Latent-to-4D surpasses matched same-latent Wan+4RC cascades in projection-based DINO-F1 by 2.88--3.45 and 5.81 points, respectively, while also being preferred by human raters for geometry, temporal stability, and overall quality.

</details>


### [62] [Where To Look? : Causal Tracing of Vision Encoders in VLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10758)
*Naren Kumar S,Tirth Bhatt,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过因果追踪（causal tracing）方法研究视觉-语言模型（VLM）中视觉信息对回答的驱动作用，发现高因果性的视觉token往往位于目标区域之外，且该模式在多个模型和不同扰动设置下一致存在。研究表明强多模态性能并不必然意味着空间局部化的因果表征，并揭示了"看见"、"使用"和"推理"视觉结构之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型虽然能高精度描述图像，但究竟哪些视觉信息真正驱动了模型的回答，这一根本问题尚未得到解答。理解视觉信息的因果路径对于模型的可靠性和可解释性至关重要。

Method: 采用因果追踪（causal tracing）方法系统地定位对模型输出具有高因果影响力的视觉token；通过在多个视觉-语言模型和多种扰动设置下进行扩展分析，验证因果模式的普遍性；并通过移除外观线索来测试模型是否仍能保留和利用视觉结构信息。

Result: 发现高因果视觉token常常位于目标区域之外；该模式在更大规模的视觉-语言模型及不同扰动设置下具有一致性；模型在缺少外观线索时仍会利用视觉线索来理解视觉结构。

Conclusion: 模型在"看见"（感知视觉信息）、"使用"（利用视觉信息）和"推理"（对视觉结构进行推理）之间存在显著差距；研究者由此提供了一个因果框架，用于研究现代视觉-语言模型如何转化、保留和利用视觉信息。

Abstract: Vision-language models can describe an image with remarkable accuracy, yet a more fundamental question remains unanswered: what visual information actually drives their answers? In this work, we investigate this question through causal tracing, and we observe that highly causal vision tokens often lie outside the target region. Extending the analysis to larger vision-language models reveals a similar pattern across models and corruption settings, suggesting that strong multimodal performance does not necessarily imply spatially localized causal representations. We further investigate: can these models preserve visual structure when appearance cues are removed? and find that visual cues are exploited to understand visual structures. Together, our experiments expose a gap between seeing, using, and reasoning over visual structure, and provide a causal framework for studying how visual information is transformed, preserved, and ultimately used by modern vision-language models.

</details>


### [63] [FADE: From Passive Verification to Active Discovery in Counterfactual Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10764)
*Fufangchen Zhao,Jinhu Fu,Jiachen Lei,Jiahong Wu,Xiangxiang Chu,Danfeng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FADE框架，用于反事实视频理解中的证据发现与解释。框架采用"证据优先"的两阶段训练范式：先进行证据内化的监督微调，再通过"渐褪锚点"强化学习逐步移除文本引导，促使模型独立发现并解释证据。同时提出将现有MCQ数据集转换为对齐MCQ、开放问答和描述生成任务的管线。基于Qwen3-VL-8B，FADE在DualityVidQA-test和IPV-Bench上均取得SOTA严格配对分数，超越GPT-5.6，且在从受限MCQ转向非受限OQA和描述生成时展现出显著的性能保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实视频理解多项选择题基准存在关键缺陷：问题与候选选项会无意中泄露目标事件，将核心挑战从主动发现降级为文本引导的验证。这使得模型无需真正理解物理和常识规律，仅靠题干信息即可作答，无法有效评估模型的因果关系理解能力。

Method: FADE采用证据优先的两个阶段训练：第一阶段为证据内化的监督微调，将模型的预测锚定在决定性视觉异常上；第二阶段采用渐褪锚点强化学习策略，逐步减少文本引导，迫使模型独立发现和解释证据。同时构建了将现有MCQ数据集转换为三种对齐任务（MCQ、开放问答、描述生成）的管线，无需额外数据筛选。

Result: 基于Qwen3-VL-8B，FADE在DualityVidQA-test和IPV-Bench三个任务上均达到最先进的严格配对分数，超越GPT-5.6。在从MCQ转向OQA和描述生成任务时，FADE的性能保持率分别为90.4%和67.4%，远超GPT-5.6的48.1%和30.7%，展现出卓越的跨任务鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FADE作为一个简单有效的训练框架，成功推动了反事实视频理解从"文本引导验证"向"主动发现与解释"的转变。其证据优先的两阶段训练策略有效地增强了模型在非约束条件下的推理能力，为未来反事实视频理解研究提供了坚实的基线。

Abstract: Counterfactual video understanding evaluates whether models grasp physical and commonsense regularities. However, existing multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks inadvertently leak target events through their questions and candidate options. This reduces the core challenge from active discovery to text-guided verification. In this paper, we present FADE, an effective training framework for counterfactual discovery and explanation. Our method is built on an evidence-first, two-stage training paradigm. First, evidence-internalized supervised fine-tuning grounds the model's predictions in decisive visual anomalies. Second, we apply a fading-anchor reinforcement learning strategy that progressively removes textual guidance, compelling the model to independently discover and explain evidence. To rigorously evaluate this capability, we also introduce an effective pipeline that converts existing MCQ datasets into aligned MCQ, open-ended question answering (OQA), and captioning tasks without requiring additional data curation. Our simple approach yields strong results. Using Qwen3-VL-8B as the baseline, FADE achieves state-of-the-art strict paired scores across all three tasks on DualityVidQA-test and IPV-Bench, outperforming GPT-5.6. In specific, when transitioning from constrained MCQs to unconstrained OQA and captioning, our model demonstrates remarkable robustness. Its performance retention is 90.4% and 67.4% on DualityVidQA-test-substantially higher than the 48.1% and 30.7% retained by GPT-5.6. We hope this simple framework can serve as a solid baseline for future research in unconstrained counterfactual video understanding.

</details>


### [64] [MVTrack: Ultrafast Appearance-Free Moving Object Tracking from Compressed Bitstreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10790)
*Iñaki Erregue,Kamal Nasrollahi,Sergio Escalera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MVTrack是一种直接在H.264比特流上运行的超快速视频跟踪器，通过MVDet运动矢量场检测器与MVLink轻量级运动学关联模块的组合，在VIRAT数据集上以60倍更少的参数、40倍更少的FLOPs和8.6倍更低的CPU延迟超越了YOLO26n，证明了仅利用压缩视频数据即可实现高精度、可扩展的监控跟踪，无需像素重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频跟踪器的大规模部署受到基于RGB的目标检测器计算成本的瓶颈限制，像素级重建和检测开销巨大，需要一种更高效的替代方案。

Method: MVTrack结合了两个核心组件：MVDet，一个针对运动矢量场的轻量级检测器，以及MVLink，一个极简的运动学关联模块。整个系统直接操作H.264压缩比特流中的运动矢量数据，绕过了像素重建步骤。

Result: 在VIRAT数据集上，MVTrack在性能上超越YOLO26n，同时仅使用其1/60的参数、1/40的FLOPs，并将CPU延迟降低8.6倍。

Conclusion: 压缩视频中的运动矢量数据本身足以支持准确的、可扩展的监控跟踪任务，未来无需进行像素重建即可实现高效跟踪。

Abstract: Deploying modern video trackers at scale is bottlenecked by the computational cost of RGB-based object detectors. To this end, we present MVTrack, an ultrafast tracker for moving objects that operates directly on H.264 bitstreams. MVTrack combines MVDet, a lightweight detector for motion vector fields, with MVLink, a minimalist kinematic association module. On VIRAT, MVTrack outperforms YOLO26n while using 60$\times$ fewer parameters, requiring 40$\times$ fewer FLOPs, and reducing CPU latency by 8.6$\times$. These results demonstrate that compressed video data alone can enable accurate and scalable surveillance tracking, thereby bypassing the need for pixel reconstruction.

</details>


### [65] [E$^3$mo-Bench: A Scalable Benchmark for Multimodal Evoked and Expressed Emotion Understanding via Bayesian Pairwise Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10796)
*Lancheng Gao,Ziheng Jia,Shengyan Li,Zixuan Xing,Jiarui Wang,Huiyu Duan,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出E³mo-Bench，一个包含12,314个问答对、覆盖2,524个视频的可扩展多模态情感基准，通过3个互补任务（情感感知、开放词汇识别、VAD评估）同时评价诱发情绪和表达情绪的理解能力；并提出贝叶斯成对对齐方法和E³mo-Score无训练代理框架来提高VAD标注的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么孤立地考察诱发情绪或表达情绪，要么局限于粗粒度、不完整的情感刻画；多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）需要同时理解表达情绪和诱发情绪才能实现全面、情感感知的交互，但缺乏相应的综合评估基准。

Method: 构建E³mo-Bench基准，包含3个互补任务（情绪感知、开放词汇识别、效价-唤醒-支配VAD评估）；提出贝叶斯成对对齐方法，将稀疏的低负担成对判断聚合成锚定参考的VAD估计；开发E³mo-Score，一种无训练代理，聚合五模型委员会的互补判断以改进VAD估计。

Result: 大量实验验证了框架的有效性，揭示了诱发情绪与表达情绪范式之间显著的性能差距，并表明MLLMs在细粒度情感识别和维度评估上仍存在持续缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究发现为推进多模态情感智能指明了清晰方向，即需要同时弥补诱发-表达情感范式间的性能不均衡，并加强MLLMs在细粒度识别与维度评估方面的能力。

Abstract: Understanding both expressed and evoked emotions is critical for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to achieve comprehensive affect-aware interactions. However, existing benchmarks typically examine expressed and evoked emotions in isolation or are constrained to coarse-grained and incomplete affective characterizations. To bridge this gap, we introduce E$^3$mo-Bench, a scalable benchmark comprising $12{,}314$ question-answer pairs across $2{,}524$ videos with predefined affective perspectives. It evaluates evoked and expressed emotion understanding via $3$ complementary tasks: emotion perception, open-vocabulary recognition, and valence-arousal-dominance (VAD) assessment. To efficiently scale reliable continuous annotations, we propose Bayesian Pairwise Alignment, which aggregates sparse, low-burden pairwise judgments into anchor-referenced VAD estimates. Furthermore, we develop E$^3$mo-Score, a training-free agent that aggregates complementary judgments from a five-model committee to improve VAD estimation. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our framework and expose a pronounced performance skew between evoked and expressed emotion paradigms. These findings, coupled with MLLMs' persistent deficits in fine-grained recognition and dimensional assessment, chart a clear course for advancing multimodal emotional intelligence.

</details>


### [66] [Beyond Fixed Luminance: Towards Panchromatic and Orthochromatic Image Colorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10798)
*Swarnim Maheshwari,Syed Imam Ali,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种与亮度无关的（luminance-agnostic）图像着色框架，将着色问题重构为基于基础图像编辑模型的全RGB图像编辑任务。通过混合灰度目标（标准亮度灰度+红不敏感灰度），同时适配现代全色与历史正色摄影条件。在COCO、ImageNet等多基准上，标准灰度下具竞争力，正色灰度下显著更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像着色系统在Lab空间固定保留输入亮度通道L，仅预测色度ab。此设计有两个缺陷：其一，固定亮度限制了着色时对亮度变化的调整能力；其二，当灰度图像的形成过程偏离自然图像亮度（如历史正色摄影对红光谱不敏感）时，模型预测会变得不可靠。

Method: 提出亮度无关着色框架：将着色任务转为基于基础图像编辑模型（foundation image-editing model）的全RGB图像编辑问题，而非在Lab空间预测色度；引入混合灰度目标，同时使用标准亮度灰度与红不敏感灰度两种灰度化方式训练模型，以桥接现代全色与历史正色两种成像条件。

Result: 在COCO、ImageNet及多实例基准上，该方法在标准灰度输入下与现有方法性能相当，而在正色灰度输入下鲁棒性显著提升；定性比较与人工研究显示，该方法产生的可见颜色伪影更少。

Conclusion: 亮度无关的全RGB编辑式着色框架能有效克服传统固定亮度着色的局限性，尤其在灰度形成机制偏离自然亮度（如历史正色摄影）的条件下表现更优，为跨成像条件的着色问题提供了一种更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Most image colorization systems operate in $Lab$ space by predicting chroma ($ab$) while preserving an input-derived luminance channel ($L$). While effective on standard benchmarks, this fixed-luminance design restricts brightness changes and becomes unreliable when grayscale formation deviates from natural-image luminance, as in historical orthochromatic photography. We propose a luminance-agnostic colorization framework that formulates colorization as full-RGB image editing using a foundation image-editing model. To bridge modern panchromatic and historical orthochromatic conditions, we introduce a mixed grayscale objective that trains the model under both standard luminance grayscale and a red-insensitive grayscale formation. Experiments on COCO, ImageNet, and a multi-instance benchmark show that our method is competitive on standard grayscale inputs and substantially more robust under orthochromatic inputs, with qualitative comparisons and a human study indicating fewer visible color artifacts.

</details>


### [67] [Evaluating Semantic and Spatial Guidance for Foundation Model Segmentation of Small-Scale PV in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10801)
*Roni Blushtein-Livnon,Tal Svoray,Osher Rafaeli,Michael Dorman,Itay Fischhendler,Havazelet Yahel,Emir Galilee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了SAM3视觉语言基础模型在不同提示策略（文本、几何、混合）下的小规模光伏分割性能，发现提示策略是决定模型行为的主导因素：文本提示性能最差且对成像条件敏感，空间引导显著提升分割精度与鲁棒性，混合提示取得最佳性能与稳定性，且仅需数百个标注样本即可获得大部分性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离网地区的光伏时空数据对理解采用过程至关重要，但此类数据基本不可用。虽然自动化遥感图像分割提供了解方案，但居民光伏系统因尺寸小、分布稀疏导致严重的目标-背景不平衡。视觉语言基础模型提供了基于提示的数据高效范式，但不同提示类型的相对贡献尚不清楚。

Method: 系统评估SAM3在遥感图像中对小规模光伏分割的性能，对比文本提示、几何提示和混合提示三种方式，在不同监督水平、训练策略、空间分辨率和成像条件下进行多维度实验。研究以大型离网农村地区的多时相航空影像为研究区，并在三个额外数据集上验证发现。

Result: 提示策略是控制模型行为的主导因素。文本提示性能最低且对监督和成像条件最敏感；空间引导显著提升分割精度和鲁棒性；混合提示达到最高精度和稳定性，表明语义和空间引导提供互补信息。大部分性能增益仅需几百个标注样本即可实现，迁移学习总体影响有限，仅在有限监督下对文本提示有适度改进。

Conclusion: 研究确立了提示策略是SAM3适应、鲁棒性和泛化的关键决定因素，凸显了可提示基础模型在数据受限的离网地区进行可扩展光伏制图的潜力。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal PV data are essential for understanding adoption processes in off-grid regions, yet such data remain largely unavailable. Automated segmentation of remote sensing (RS) imagery offers a promising solution; yet, residential PV systems remain challenging targets because of their small size and sparse distribution, resulting in severe target-background imbalance. Vision-language foundation models (FMs) provide a data-efficient paradigm through prompt-based semantic and spatial guidance, but the relative contribution of different prompt types remains unclear. We systematically evaluate SAM3 for small-scale PV segmentation in RS imagery by comparing textual, geometric, and hybrid prompting, under varying supervision levels, training strategies, spatial resolutions, and imaging conditions. Multi-temporal aerial imagery from a large off-grid rural region serves as a study site, with findings validated across three additional datasets. Prompting strategy emerged as the dominant factor governing model behavior. Textual prompting consistently produced the lowest performance and showed the greatest sensitivity to supervision and imaging conditions. In contrast, spatial guidance substantially improved both segmentation accuracy and robustness. Hybrid prompting achieved the highest accuracy and stability, indicating that semantic and spatial guidance provide complementary information. Most performance gains were achieved with only a few hundred annotated samples, demonstrating strong data efficiency. Transfer learning had limited overall impact, with only modest improvements observed for textual prompting under limited supervision. Overall, our findings establish prompting strategy as a key determinant of SAM3 adaptation, robustness, and generalization, highlighting the potential of promptable FMs for scalable PV mapping in data-constrained off-grid regions.

</details>


### [68] [BPG: Balancing Plasticity and Generalization for Domain Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10804)
*Qiang Wang,Songlin Dong,Shaokun Wang,Jizhou Han,Xiang Song,Chenhao Ding,Yuhang He,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BPG框架，通过动态确定适配器隐藏维度和测试时软域混合策略，提升域增量学习性能，在DomainNet、CDDB和CORe50上达到SOTA平均准确率，遗忘率低至0.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数隔离范式的域增量学习方法采用一刀切策略适应新域，导致学习能力不足或参数冗余的问题。

Method: BPG由两个组件构成：BPG-Adapter基于域特定的特征可分性动态确定每个域的适配器隐藏维度；BPG-Inference采用软域混合策略在测试时集成多个域特定模型，缓解域ID误选问题。

Result: 在DomainNet、CDDB和CORe50数据集上，BPG一致优于均匀适配器方法和硬域选择策略，达到SOTA平均准确率，在DomainNet上将遗忘率降至0.22%。

Conclusion: BPG通过动态适配容量分配和软域混合推理，有效解决了参数隔离类DIL方法中学习容量不足与参数冗余的矛盾，同时缓解了域ID误选问题。

Abstract: Deep neural networks excel in various tasks but struggle to generalize across evolving data distributions, leading to significant performance degradation under domain shifts. Domain incremental learning (DIL) addresses this challenge by enabling models to continuously adapt while retaining prior knowledge. Among existing DIL approaches, the parameter-isolation paradigm achieves state-of-the-art performance. However, these methods often adopt a one-size-fits-all approach to adapt to new domains, resulting in either insufficient learning capacity or redundant parameters. In this work, we propose BPG, a unified framework that addresses both challenges through two complementary components: BPG-Adapter, which dynamically determines each domain's adapter hidden dimension based on domain-specific feature separability, and BPG-Inference, a soft domain mixture strategy that integrates multiple domain-specific models at test time, mitigating domain ID misselection. Experimental results on DomainNet, CDDB, and CORe50 demonstrate that BPG consistently outperforms uniform adapter-based approaches and hard domain selection strategies, achieving state-of-the-art average accuracy while reducing forgetting to as low as 0.22% on DomainNet.

</details>


### [69] [Fast and Memory-Efficient Wavelet Convolutions via I/O-Aware Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10805)
*Amit Aflalo,Shahaf E. Finder,Roy Amoyal,Eran Treister,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对小波卷积（WTConv）参考实现因过度HBM数据移动而严重内存受限的问题，通过I/O模型分析瓶颈，提出三种代数重构（芯片上重算Haar分析、将多级合成级联折叠为按输出坐标比特索引的闭式单pass、将学习到的逐通道缩放折叠进卷积权重），实现I/O感知的融合实现，将HBM流量减少约2.55倍，训练速度提升最高4.35倍，峰值内存约减半，同时保留WTConv的全部优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WTConv作为标准卷积的即插即用替代品，能以线性参数扩展指数级感受野，但参考实现因过度的HBM数据移动而严重受限于内存带宽，导致实际执行效率远低于其理论优势，亟需解决系统性开销以释放其潜力。

Method: 首先建立WTConv的I/O模型以刻画内存瓶颈，据此指导三种代数重构：(1)在片上重算廉价Haar分析蝶形变换，避免从HBM读回中间结果；(2)将多级合成级联折叠为按输出坐标比特索引的单一闭式pass，消除级间数据往返；(3)将可学习的逐通道缩放因子折叠进卷积权重中，减少额外的内存访存。最终组合为I/O感知的融合内核实现。

Result: 在WTConvNeXt配置下跨不同分解层次和张量形状评估：模型化HBM流量减少约2.55倍，训练相比参考实现加速最高4.35倍，峰值内存使用约减半，同时保持可比的算术运算量。
